Publication Date: 1756

Source: Internet Archive item twofirst00whit (https://archive.org/details/twofirst00whit), the item's own twofirst00whit_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1756. Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Princeton Theological Seminary scan; readable with the long s rendered as f; a bequest plate precedes the title page and is left in place.
Content note: Whitefield's own account of his early life and his Journals of 1737-1741, as he himself revised and abridged them in 1756; the revival's other founder in his own words, including the Georgia voyages and the first field preaching.
